# Omissions and source gaps

The complete original W1 native session is not established; retained item-array exports provide an available subset. Historical missing sandbox attachments/citation targets remain marked in their messages. Windows raw workspace packages remain privately held. Encrypted client payloads, unavailable capture tails and original private image pixels are not reconstructed.

The entries below inventory omitted source files without exposing original filenames, paths or checksums. A metadata-only item is not represented as an available conversation.
